**URL:** https://coreweave.com/ai-cloud-horizons

AI Cloud Horizons Series | CoreWeave WEBCAST SERIES AI Cloud Horizons Expert insights on the future of GPU Infrastructure From benchmark results to real-world TCO, get a front-row seat to the conversations shaping enterprise AI infrastructure. Get Early Access Be the first to know when new episodes drop. Get expert insights before anyone else. Text Link Episode 4 NOW AVAILABLE Inside IBM Granite 4.0: How This Pioneering Model Powers Enterprise AI IBM Granite 4.0 is pioneering what’s possible for enterprise AI. ‍ Join IBM’s Brian Belgodere and CoreWeave’s Navarre Pratt as they explore the trials and triumphs of training entirely on NVIDIA GB200 NVL72 infrastructure—one of the first deployments of its kind. Discover what this breakthrough means for speed, scalability, and innovation for teams looking to accelerate their AI roadmaps. How IBM and CoreWeave built one of the first NVIDIA GB200 NVL72-powered AI supercomputers How IBM Granite 4.0 enables faster model development and open-source flexibility for enterprises Lessons from solving first-of-its-kind challenges in rack power density, cooling, and observability at scale Episode 1 Live Burn-In to Breakthrough: Insights from Operating Large Scale AI Clusters What makes a GPU cloud truly enterprise-ready? Join CoreWeave CTO Peter Salanki in conversation with Dylan Patel, the lead analyst behind the groundbreaking SemiAnalysis ClusterMAX™ report. In this podcast, they break down what sets GPU cloud providers apart—and what it means for enterprises training and deploying the next wave of AI models. They also talk about the recent NVIDIA GB200 deployments, the challenges of scaling, and more. Watch now Watch now Episode 2 Live The SLA is Not Enough: Redefining Reliability for AI Infrastructure SLAs look good on paper, but when a single GPU node goes down mid-training, what happens next? In this session, we’ll explore why traditional SLAs fail to meet the needs of distributed AI workloads—and how modern GPU clouds are redefining what reliability really means. Learn how CoreWeave treats the SLA as a living operational framework, not just a legal checkbox. Watch now Watch now Episode 3 Live Beyond $/GPU-Hour: How to Really Measure TCO for AI Cloud How do enterprises calculate the total cost of ownership (TCO) for GPU Clouds? Join Urvashi Chowdhary, Head of Product at CoreWeave, for a deep dive into the real drivers of GPU cloud TCO. We’ll go beyond surface-level pricing to explore the technical and operational decisions that shape cost, efficiency, and performance.
